Now evaluate attacker types:

1) Attacker lanceiro (T_att=lanceiro):
- Does defender counter attacker? Defender lanceiro counters attacker? Defender counters attacker if T_def counters T_att. Does lanceiro counter lanceiro? No (same type not a counter). So defender does NOT counter attacker.
Thus def_eff = def_base *1.25 =6*1.25=7.5.
- Does attacker counter defender? Attacker lanceiro counters defender if T_att counters T_def. Lanceiro counters cavaleiro only. So vs lanceiro defender, no counter.
Thus att_eff = att_atk = a*1 = a.
Need a > 7.5 => a >=8 lanceiros.

2) Attacker arqueiro (T_att=arqueiro):
- Defender counters attacker? Defender lanceiro counters arqueiro? Yes, arqueiro counters lanceiro, so defender lanceiro counters attacker arqueiro. So defender counters attacker.
Thus def_eff = def_base *1.5625 =6*1.5625=9.375.
- Attacker counters defender? Arqueiro counters lanceiro? Yes, arqueiro counters lanceiro. So attacker gets counter multiplier.
Thus att_eff = (a*2) *1.25 = a*2.5.
Need a*2.5 >9.375 => a > 3.75 => a >=4 arqueiros.

3) Attacker cavaleiro (T_att=cavaleiro):
- Defender counters attacker? Defender lanceiro counters cavaleiro? Lanceiro counters cavaleiro, so yes defender counters attacker.
Thus def_eff = def_base *1.5625 =9.375 (same as above).
- Attacker counters defender? Cavaleiro counters lanceiro? No, cavaleiro counters arqueiro. So vs lanceiro defender, attacker does NOT counter.
Thus att_eff = a*4 (no multiplier).
Need a*4 >9.375 => a >2.34375 => a >=3 cavaleiros.

Thus for [19], options: 8 lanceiros, 4 arqueiros, 3 cavaleiros.
